## P2 N3–N10: actual-source assessment, not blanket acceptance

- **N3 FIXED/tested.** Explicit data-root or PALACE_DATA_ROOT skips unrelated home runtime discovery; managed-path inference still enforces disabled/uninstalled/faulted/upgrade restrictions, and database symlink refusal remains. Separate archive has null runtime/write authority.
- **N4 CONFIRMED OPEN.** `PluginHost.markFault` still swallows save rejection and `load` can reload old registry. Simply setting shared pending flag is insufficient: direct load calls and external newer revision need per-fault reconciliation without overriding explicit enable intent. Registry atomic rename lacks POSIX parent-directory fsync. Next grouped lifecycle-persistence fix needs injected failure + direct reload + revision-race tests before implementation, plus platform-specific fsync evidence. No policy acceptance granted.
- **N5 CONFIRMED OPEN.** Fallback snapshot uses mkdtemp `.schema-probe-*` and only finally removes it. Process death bypasses finally. Do not blindly delete all matching directories on startup: another schema probe may still be active, and prefix alone proves no ownership. Needs owned marker/namespace/birth or an equally safe coordination design, killed-probe and active-peer preservation tests, backup interaction test. No cleanup was added.
- **N6 CONFIRMED OPEN.** Faulted lifecycle branch regexes diagnostic text and always recommends bundled upgrade for presumed missing entry. Needs actual entry inspection and installationSource-aware command preserving manual provenance; test misleading ENOENT unrelated to entry and manually registered missing entry. No automatic provenance switch authorized.
- **N7 CONFIRMED OPEN.** bundleUpgradeRequired remediation omits agent-stop warning. Skill wording says reads are read-only while active (too narrow), and standalone-only has no explicit no-write-path wording. Skills already dirty from prior writer. Defer to grouped remediation/docs change with regression checks; do not treat old real-agent Skill hash as current acceptance. New manifest records actual Skill bytes.
- **N8 CONFIRMED SOURCE MISMATCH; reproduction OPEN.** Full context canonicalizes package root via realpath and home recursively; quick probe uses path.resolve for both, including target ledger lookups. Aliased prefix/home can yield false missing/update. Needs junction/symlink fixture (Windows junction where no symlink privilege) and observational no-write/no-spawn checks. Do not add broad owner tree verification to the hot path.
- **N9 CONFIRMED OPEN.** postinstall service-profile guard is only in `scripts/v12/install-palace-skill.cjs`; startup directly calls reconcilePalaceSkill, which lacks equivalent Windows guard. Move/shared guard must precede context filesystem writes, preserve intentional explicit-home/force policy, test direct reconciler under SYSTEM with home unchanged. Do not simulate by modifying real service identity.
- **N10 CONFIRMED OPEN.** ProcessDriver drops response for cancelled target after clearing cancellation supervision, including PLUGIN_* error. Needs child fixture proving delayed PLUGIN_* faults while ordinary CANCELLED remains non-fatal and original deadline is preserved. Use existing bounded stderr fault path; do not fault on every cancellation response.
- **plugins list side effects CONFIRMED OPEN.** load invokes recoverUpgradeIntents; list can roll back. Choose/document recovery semantics or move recovery to explicit repair in a separate tested lifecycle change. No read-only claim here.
- **legacy namespace-less lock remediation OPEN.** coordinationOwnerAbandoned correctly refuses unknown namespace. Keep refusal; add operator instructions only after verifying host/PID and identity, never instruct blind deletion of a possibly live shared-root owner.
- **permanently unsafe parent / ExperimentalWarning OPEN.** unsafe-parent is reported by full inspect, quick probe does not guarantee that state. Preserve user files and require explicit corrective layout; do not suppress SQLite experimental warnings globally merely for clean stderr.

## Changes and rationale

- `src/platform/plugin-host.ts`: retains per-plugin unpersisted fault snapshots across direct load and unrelated newer registry revisions; refresh retries the reconciled fault instead of resurrecting a child. Explicit enable/disable carry a fresh optional persisted lifecycleToken, allowing identical-state enable to supersede a startup fault without mistaking unrelated revisions for recovery. Uninstall flows through disable. Changed records/generations supersede the pending snapshot; same activation intent normalizes only installed/enabled transient state. Fault-save failures set retry state. Save success clears pending snapshots.
- Registry save now fsyncs the parent directory on POSIX after atomic rename. In-memory revision advances immediately after rename, even if directory sync fails, so retries do not conflict with their own published revision. Handle closes in finally; failures remain observable. Windows directory handles are not opened. No claim of actual Linux filesystem durability acceptance: the new fault-injection regression exercises the POSIX branch by overriding platform and intercepting only directory open on Windows.
- `src/platform/plugin-driver.ts`: cancelled requests retain their stderr sequence; a late PLUGIN_* response enters the existing 40ms-quiet /1000ms-maximum stderr fault path. Ordinary CANCELLED remains nonfatal and a subsequent real ping succeeds. Original request deadline/grace calculation unchanged. Existing stderr tail remains4096 characters (not a new UTF8-byte cap).
- New `test/v12/d1-lifecycle-persistence.test.mjs` covers failed fault save/direct load/unrelated revision/restart with child PID gone, combined activation+fault persistence failures, explicit newer enable/disable/uninstall, and post-rename directory-sync failure revision/handle behavior.
- New `test/v12/d1-cancelled-fault.test.mjs` uses a real executable JSON-lines child to return delayed fault/CANCELLED after cancellation; asserts late bounded diagnostic tail and continued usability for ordinary cancellation.

Exact changed file bytes/hashes: `changed-files.json`. Only these four files were changed by this writer. Source files already contained inherited dirty WIP; HEAD diff is not this writer's delta. Pre-write copies are private in the isolated working directory. `inherited-source-comparison.json` checks262 non-owned prior manifest files: **zero drift**.

===== BEGIN EVIDENCE E:\git-project\agent-mcp-device-v12\evidence\v12-e2e-20261024-g6\D1-lifecycle-fixes-20261028\review-final.md | original-bytes=1736 | sha256=424a3ecc9bf6d82995b56ed7c21a46b8aa5153981738614bae1b6cf494889e99 =====
**No concrete remaining issues found in the reviewed delta. Prior finding is addressed.**

- **Sticky fault after failed activation/fault saves:** `src/platform/plugin-host.ts:174–189` reconciles pending faults across `installed`/`enabled` differences while requiring all other fields—including `lifecycleToken`—to match. Failed fault saves retain retry state at `src/platform/plugin-host.ts:400–404`; refresh retries reconciled faults at `src/platform/plugin-host.ts:428–432`.
- **Newer explicit intent wins:** enable/disable assign fresh lifecycle tokens at `src/platform/plugin-host.ts:447–450`, so reconciliation rejects stale pending faults rather than overriding a newer same-state enable.
- **Persistence publication:** `src/platform/plugin-host.ts:216–224` updates the in-memory revision immediately after rename, before POSIX directory sync; a sync failure therefore does not leave retries using the pre-publication revision.
- **Cancelled faults / bounded stderr:** cancelled `PLUGIN_*` responses enter fault handling at `src/platform/plugin-driver.ts:78–85`. Diagnostics retain a 4,096-character tail (`src/platform/plugin-driver.ts:99`), with a 40 ms quiet period and 1,000 ms maximum wait (`src/platform/plugin-driver.ts:181–205`); ordinary `CANCELLED` responses do not fault the driver.

Read both requested test files: the persistence scenarios are covered in `test/v12/d1-lifecycle-persistence.test.mjs:25–81`; delayed cancelled-fault classification and diagnostic bounds in `test/v12/d1-cancelled-fault.test.mjs:9–25`.

Static review only, against the dirty working tree at HEAD `18407ec562c4f2cc11ca3e8c1f69f362ee3f86c0`. No edits, tests, builds, or network activity; **not acceptance evidence**.
===== END EVIDENCE E:\git-project\agent-mcp-device-v12\evidence\v12-e2e-20261024-g6\D1-lifecycle-fixes-20261028\review-final.md =====

===== BEGIN EVIDENCE E:\git-project\agent-mcp-device-v12\evidence\v12-e2e-20261024-g6\D1-lifecycle-fixes-20261028\review-initial.md | original-bytes=1134 | sha256=ce869408db21c15be91929dd1c47b22dba9b047e91256dec86eb92287f16f24b =====
- **P2 — Pending fault is discarded when the preceding healthy-state save also failed.** `src/platform/plugin-host.ts:176-182` preserves a fault only if the disk record exactly matches its captured pre-fault or faulted record. But activation sets the in-memory state to `enabled` before saving, and explicitly tolerates save failure (`src/platform/plugin-host.ts:374-380`). If that save fails, then the driver faults and fault persistence also fails, `markFault()` captures `enabled` as `previous` (`src/platform/plugin-host.ts:397`), while disk still contains `installed`. A direct `load()` therefore deletes the pending fault and restores `installed`; an unrelated external revision can subsequently cause refresh to reactivate the faulted plugin without explicit enable. Preserve the last persisted baseline or reconcile using lifecycle intent/generation rather than exact transient-state equality. Add a regression covering failed activation save → failed fault save → direct load/unrelated revision.

No other actionable issue found in the focused delta. Read-only review; no tests, builds, benchmarks, or network executed.
===== END EVIDENCE E:\git-project\agent-mcp-device-v12\evidence\v12-e2e-20261024-g6\D1-lifecycle-fixes-20261028\review-initial.md =====

**No concrete correctness or safety blockers found in the bounded N5–N9 delta.**

Reviewed only the specified source ranges, Skill guidance, remaining-regression tests, crashed-snapshot addition, and isolated Device A5 shell flow. Confirmed HEAD is `18407ec562c4f2cc11ca3e8c1f69f362ee3f86c0`; the checkout contains substantial inherited WIP, which was not treated as this delta.

**Nonblocking limitations**
- `src/plugins/palace/store.ts:13–31`: Cleanup deliberately preserves snapshots with uncertain ownership, different namespaces, live/reused PIDs, or unexpected contents. Some abandoned copies may therefore require manual cleanup; active peers are conservatively preserved.
- `src/plugins/palace/skill-manager.ts:545–590`: The quick probe remains ledger-only and observational. Canonical path agreement does not imply fresh verification of installed Skill bytes; the returned verification label accurately reflects this.
- `test/v12/d1-remaining-regressions.test.mjs:41–51`: Service-account coverage simulates Windows platform/account values; it does not establish behavior under native Windows service-token/profile conditions.
- `test/v12/palace-wal-recovery.test.mjs:48–62`: The crashed-snapshot regression forces the recovery fallback through a loader shim. It exercises real snapshot creation, cleanup, and active-peer preservation, but is not native ACL/mount-failure acceptance.
- `test/v12/device-cold-local.test.mjs:1–2`, `test/v12/device-cold-local.test.mjs:59`: A5 exercises the isolated real Device shell adapter with an inert Gateway transport—not live Gateway or rollout acceptance.

No files modified and no tests/builds executed. The reported **58/58**, **3/3**, and enabled repeat **1/1** remain parent-provided execution evidence, not independently rerun results.

    ---
    name: palace-cli
    description: Use Palace's local CLI to retrieve evidence-backed project or device knowledge, check prior technical decisions, inspect procedure candidates, and avoid repeating previously observed errors. Apply before broad workspace/archive/log inspection when a task mentions an incident, error code, failed behavior, regression, upgrade/cancellation/schema/SQLite/runtime/Skill problem that may have happened before, or asks for historical context or a reusable fix. Do not recall for self-contained formatting/limits or clearly novel work without a history signal. Use the installed Palace CLI and never call a dedicated Palace MCP server or NeuralMemory MCP tools.
    ---
    
    # Palace CLI — evidence-first recall
    
    Palace is an optional plugin, enabled by default with MCP Device or installed separately as `@hcu-lab.me/palace`. Its local **CLI**, not an MCP knowledge endpoint, is the operational interface.
    
    ## Availability first
    
    Always begin with:
    
    ```bash
    palace status
    ```
    
    If `palace` is not on `PATH`, report that Palace is not installed. Do not run a source-checkout file as a substitute. If status reports `PALACE_DISABLED`, `PALACE_UNINSTALLED`, `PALACE_FAULTED`, `PALACE_UPGRADE_REQUIRED`, or another limitation, report its remediation and do not use Palace for the task.
    
    ## Retrieval commands
    
    Run on the device hosting the Palace database:
    
    ```bash
    palace search "specific problem or error" --limit 3 --max-bytes 8000
    palace search "project issue" --project-id PROJECT_ID --limit 3
    palace read CARD_ID --max-bytes 16000
    palace skills                         # validated procedures only
    palace skills --include-candidates    # candidates are not approved instructions
    ```
    
    When needed, pass `--runtime-dir /path/to/device/runtime` or an explicitly reviewed `--data-root /path/to/palace/data`. Without an explicit runtime, Palace resolves the active Device/server runtime and refuses ambiguous roots. For agents on another machine, use an **existing authorized Device shell tool** to run `palace` on the target machine. Do not introduce a Palace-specific MCP server.
    
    ## Workflow
    
    1. Run `palace status` and obey the returned runtime/plugin state.
    2. For an incident/error/failure/regression/repair that may have happened before, run one bounded `palace search` on the exact code or distinguishing symptom **before** broad workspace/archive/log inspection. If nothing relevant is returned, or only stale/out-of-scope material appears, abstain and continue normal investigation; do not keep searching Palace.
    3. Inspect each result's `status`, `scope`, `evidenceCount`, `version`, and `summary`. If an exact `validated` procedure matches the current preconditions, read only that card and avoid a broad archive scan that merely rediscovers the same procedure. Verify the current target/postcondition independently, and inspect only the specific original evidence needed for any claim that is not already established by the validated procedure.
    4. Treat `hypothesis` and `observed` as leads, not validated facts. A read-only snapshot does not reverify original source bytes; inspect primary evidence before relying on a claim.
    5. Use `palace skills` without `--include-candidates` for reusable procedures. `activationPolicy: candidate_only` means this runtime cannot validate new candidates; it does not invalidate previously recorded validations. Never treat a candidate as an approved procedure.
    6. Keep every Palace/read/process-output chunk at or below 16 KiB. Use `--max-bytes`, bounded result counts, exact line/range reads, or multiple small chunks; never request an entire large log or process transcript just to find one fact.
    7. If evidence disagrees with the real environment, prefer direct observation and report the mismatch.
    8. Report precise provenance and keep the final answer compact.
    
    ## Mutation and validation boundary
    
    Palace knowledge operations (`status/search/read/skills`) are read-only while the runtime is active. `palace skill install|repair|remove|adopt` changes only the agent Skill installation and ownership ledger; it does not mutate Palace knowledge. Corrections and new procedures use the authorized `mcp-device knowledge correct|learn --runtime-dir DIR` workflow while that runtime is stopped under its exclusive lease. Never stop a runtime unless the user explicitly requests it. Never claim that a candidate is validated without a trusted execution receipt and independent postcondition.
    
    ## Boundaries
    
    - Do not call NeuralMemory or legacy `knowledge.*` MCP tools for Palace operations.
    - Never infer `validated` from similarity, repetition, model text, or a successful shell exit alone.
    - Do not expose confidential card bodies or logs beyond the current request's scope.
    - Verify token savings with completed-task correctness and actual provider token/billing telemetry against a no-Palace control under the same model and task conditions.

# Repository agent contract — MCP Device

**Read [docs/STATUS.md](docs/STATUS.md) before modifying source or choosing a branch.** It defines canonical Device/Gateway checkouts, release-vs-runtime distinctions, retained active work, and safety gates. [docs/ARCHIVE.md](docs/ARCHIVE.md) explains how to retrieve DONE/outdated work through Git history or private backups.

- Verify `git rev-parse --show-toplevel HEAD`, branch, dirty/untracked/ignored state and process/CWD on the actual device. Do not treat worktree path or branch name alone as authority.
- Device and Gateway are separate products. The Device source contains Context v1.0.11, but Gateway `develop` does **not** yet expose the Gateway Context patch. Never merge that patch without an explicit task.
- Do not `reset --hard`, clean files, remove worktrees forcibly, force-push, reload production, mutate identity/SQLite or publish/tag as a side effect of documentation or spec work.
- **Active development:** `specs/006-agent-memory-palace/`. G6 P1 implements local `mcp-device logs` search/read/activity graph from **existing** Pi/Codex/MCP files, not live capture. Wiki, cross-device recall and lazy auto-merged skills are future phases. Historic 003–005 are retired from HEAD.
- **Handoff required:** read `specs/006-agent-memory-palace/HANDOFF.md` before changing this feature. Do not reactivate legacy observer/capture or add an extra permission/redaction gate to authorized local log indexing. Use actual repo/test evidence and small commits.
- Keep `specs/002-device-local-context/` (DONE, required regression fixture), accepted evidence and executable benchmark core; do not mistake ignored caches for source.
- Do not require human approval for ordinary agent-owned skill creation/consolidation when 006 is later implemented; keep automatic tests, scope security, versioning and rollback. This spec cleanup creates no scheduler.
- Record expected/actual tests, exact SHA and authorization. Distinguish historical acceptance, fresh measurement, current source and proposals; no tool call or rollout claim without evidence.

# Current status — MCP Device 1.0.12

Updated 2026-10-22. This is the v12 source candidate on `feat/v12-platform-20261022` in the isolated G6 checkout `E:\git-project\agent-mcp-device-v12`. The original Device and Gateway checkouts remain separate. No npm publication, production deployment, global Device replacement or G8 runtime change is claimed.

## Current increment

- Existing `start` remains Device-only; `device` is an alias. `server` composes the retained account/OAuth control plane with the same Device Core in process.
- Normal account, OAuth and Device pairing apply to localhost. Operator pages share the server listener and administrator policy.
- The generic versioned plugin host supports lifecycle, dependency services, composition and failure isolation. Palace is bundled/enabled by distribution policy; Core does not depend on it.
- Platform knowledge search/list return compact references without import. Selected read can lazily import into a compatible requester provider; a Device without Palace uses bounded session cache. Ownership, scope, origin, version, evidence and correction rules remain enforced.
- Palace discovers bounded known local sources offline, constructs knowledge and inactive procedure candidates, and requires trusted execution plus postconditions for learned Skill activation. It does not rewrite AGENTS.md.
- Stopped-state backup, verification, migration and restore preserve sensitive state and plugin evidence. Apply writes only a new target after verified backups.

## Evidence and limits

[Spec 008 acceptance](../specs/008-v12-platform/ACCEPTANCE.md) owns the final test/package receipts and remaining gaps. [Device inventory](../specs/008-v12-platform/device-parity.json) and [Gateway inventory](../specs/008-v12-platform/gateway-parity.json) separate baseline evidence, candidate results and NOT_RUN/PARTIAL items. The [v12 guide](v12-platform.md) documents commands and operating constraints.

The old Windows direct-entry test guards could exit without executing assertions. V12 parity executes the actual exported suites and records assertion-bearing baseline/candidate receipts. Historic aggregate counts must not be reused as new acceptance. Known baseline fixture gaps are recorded explicitly rather than hidden by candidate-only changes.

Retrieval remains lexical. The independent paraphrase holdout, task-replay measurements, response-byte costs and unmeasured workload limits are reported separately from the tuned regression corpus. No general token-saving or production workload claim follows from the synthetic fixtures.

Only G6 isolated port 8104 is used for server acceptance; the existing 8103 audit listener is preserved. G8 8102 is production and 8101 is backup, outside this work.

## Earlier status

The [2026-10-13 P1 snapshot](status-p1-20261013.md) is retained as historical context, including claims superseded by the executable v12 parity audit. Existing P1 `logs` and gated Context behavior remain compatibility surfaces; their historical capture approvals are not changed by enabling the separate Palace plugin.

### 1. Gửi D1 fresh review qua FJP — NGAY

FJP đang online. Dùng MCP `list_devices` để lấy device_id của FJP, sau đó `shell_execute` để:

```powershell
# Trên FJP — launcher path đã biết từ skill
$M365 = Join-Path $env:USERPROFILE '.m365-copilot2dom\m365.cmd'
& $M365 doctor  # kiểm tra ready:true trước

# Nếu ready, submit D1 review
& $M365 worker submit `
  --workspace 'C:\<caller-workspace-trên-FJP>' `
  --prompt-file 'C:\<path>\d1-review-request-20261027.md' `
  --model opus
```

Prompt đã chuẩn bị tại:
```
E:/test/mcp-device-v12-e2e-20261024-g6/pi-g6-2080-20261026/d1-review-request-20261027.md
```

**Conversation cũ của D1**: turn `2e4a9646-m365-worker-20261025-164417-e6b74a82`  
→ Dùng `--conversation-url` nếu muốn tiếp tục cùng thread.

---

### 2. Trong khi đợi D1 review — sửa MF-1..MF-6

Đây là 6 lỗi phải sửa để D1 PASS. Sửa theo test-first (test đỏ trước, sửa code, test xanh).

#### MF-1: Palace CLI trỏ sai runtime khi Device chạy device-mode
**File**: `src/plugins/palace/cli.ts`, `src/platform/device-runtime.ts`  
**Fix**: Thêm `resolveRuntimeRoot()` chung — ưu tiên `--runtime-dir` → `MCP_DEVICE_CONFIG_DIR` → discovery (server root rồi device root theo active lease).  
**Test cần viết**: Cold device-only install + `palace status` → phải trả `ok` với device root.

#### MF-2: Cancel job B giết job A đang chạy
**File**: `src/platform/plugin-driver.ts`  
**Dòng**: `graceMs = min(5000, deadlineAt - now)` — bỏ `min(5000, ...)`  
**Fix**: `graceMs = deadlineAt - now` (deadline gốc, không cap)  
**Test**: Job A chạy 7s, cancel job B ở 1s → A hoàn thành, B rejected CANCELLED, plugin vẫn enabled.

#### MF-3: WAL check copy toàn bộ database ra /tmp
**File**: `src/plugins/palace/store.ts`  
**Fix**: Thay `copyFileSync` bằng `readOnly:true` connection. Chỉ snapshot trong data directory nếu `SQLITE_READONLY_RECOVERY` sau khi kiểm tra free space.  
**Test**: Writer bị kill với frames chưa checkpoint + v2 schema → main file và WAL hash không đổi, /tmp sạch, khởi động trong deadline.

#### MF-4: Registry write failure fault healthy Palace (Windows)
**File**: `src/platform/plugin-host.ts`  
**Fix**: Dùng `renameWithRetry` + fsync trong `save()`. Save failure sau `driver.start()` không được gọi `markFault`.  
**Test (Windows)**: Giữ registry.json open 300ms không share-delete trong khi server start → Palace đạt `enabled`, không fault.

#### MF-5: Fault sticky qua restart + remediation không rõ
**File**: `src/platform/plugin-host.ts`, `src/plugins/palace/cli.ts`  
**Fix**:
- Remediation text → exact command: `mcp-device plugins --runtime-dir "<root>" enable palace`  
- Giữ bounded 4KB tail của child stderr trong fault record  
- `PALACE_UPGRADE_REQUIRED` nói rõ runtime phải stop trước  
- Quyết định policy: sticky (explicit) hay auto-restart (systemd-style) — **phải chọn một và viết test**

#### MF-6: 3 test không thể fail với behavior chúng tên
**Tests cần fix**:
1. `D1 M1 temporarily absent package` → assert ngay sau khi owner parked (không chỉ final state)
2. `registry stale-lock recovery never loses a retried update` → 200 iter, không retry, mọi install phải present
3. `metadata probes 20ms p95` → spawn CLI thực 200 lần (không dùng cache), đo p95 thực

1. One correction I owe on MF-6(c)

I defined the MF-6(c) metric myself: the difference between the two arms' p95 values, each computed separately, at ≤20 ms. That estimator is defective as an acceptance instrument, and the receipts show why:

Two runs of the same code gave 18.56 ms and 30.16 ms. The run-to-run spread (11.6 ms) is more than half the threshold.
In the failing receipt, both arms show a contiguous stretch of about 20 slow samples (above ~260 ms) at the same pair positions late in the run. Even the skip arm, which does strictly less work, has p95 = 332 ms against a median of about 160 ms.
Each arm's p95 therefore lands inside a host-wide disturbance, not in the product's own cost.

This does not turn either run into a pass.

The 30.16 ms result remains the authoritative FAIL.
The earlier 18.56 ms result stays superseded.
MF-6(c) can close only with a fresh run under a protocol registered before that run (A3 below). The 20 ms threshold stays the same.
The diagnostic (in-process probe p95 9.6 ms) is not evidence in either direction.

3. New findings
Must fix before D1 PASS

N1 (P1): Windows read path starts PowerShell for every palace call while a runtime is active.

Location [S]:
runtime-root.ts activeLease() calls processBirth(pid) for every live lease.
palace/cli.ts runtimeIsActive() calls it again.
On win32, processBirth runs powershell.exe with a 3 s timeout.
With Device running and no environment variable set, a single palace status starts two PowerShell processes.
That contradicts the design we agreed in D1-R3 (no birth-identity queries on read paths). It is also the normal deployed condition, and the MF-6 benchmark (--data-root archive, no lease) never measures it.
Fix: read paths decide liveness from PID plus namespace only, and report leaseVerification:'pid-namespace'. Birth identity stays reserved for lock recovery and takeover.
A reused PID can then at worst make a stale lease look active. If the other root is truly active, that gives RUNTIME_AMBIGUOUS, which fails closed.
Remove the duplicate liveness logic in the CLI by reusing inspectRuntimeRoot.
Tests:
With a live lease held by a spawned process, count child processes spawned during palace status with a child_process hook. Expect 0.
Add an MF-6 benchmark arm with an active lease.

N2 (P1): an interrupted first initialization leaves a permanent fault.

Location: the PalaceStore constructor rejects any file with size > 0 and no meta table [S].
On first start, PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL writes page 1 before BEGIN IMMEDIATE … COMMIT. A crash, kill or ENOSPC in that window leaves a schemaless file.
Every later start then throws PALACE_SCHEMA_UNSUPPORTED. The fault is sticky, and the only recovery is deleting the file by hand [I].
Fix: treat a file with zero non-internal sqlite_master objects as fresh. Keep rejecting any file that has foreign objects.
Test: create the file, set WAL mode, create no tables, close, then construct the store. Expect it to open and reach schema 1. The existing foreign-table rejection test must still pass.
Should fix, or explicitly accept, before D1 closes (P2)
#	Location	Issue
N3	palace/cli.ts main	An explicit --data-root still runs resolveRuntimeRoot first. On a host with both registries present, archive reads fail with RUNTIME_AMBIGUOUS. Skip discovery for explicit data roots.
N4	PluginHost.markFault	save().catch(()=>{}). If persisting the fault fails, the next load() overwrites the in-memory fault, and a later revision change re-activates the plugin, so the fault is no longer sticky. Set registrySavePending there too. Registry save() also lacks a POSIX directory fsync (the skill manager has one).
N5	store fallback	A crash during the fallback snapshot leaves .schema-probe-*, containing a full copy of the database plus a non-empty -wal. It is never cleaned up, and state backup then fails with SQLITE_CHECKPOINT_REQUIRED. Remove stale owned probe directories when the store starts.
N6	CLI lifecycle() faulted branch	It detects a missing entry with a regex over the error text, not by checking whether the entry file exists. It also recommends upgrade palace --bundled for manual (standalone) registrations, which switches provenance. Branch on installationSource.
N7	remediation / SKILL.md	The PALACE_UPGRADE_REQUIRED text omits "the agent must not stop the runtime". SKILL.md still says "read-only while the runtime is active" and doesn't mention that a standalone-only install has no write path. The SKILL hash is now 4e8642d4…, so the real-agent receipt (3910c44e…) is stale either way.
N8	palaceSkillQuickProbe	Looks up path.resolve keys while the ledger stores realpath / canonical-home keys. On aliased homes or prefixes this can loop on false update-available/missing [I]. Use the same canonicalization.
N9	mcp-device.ts startup reconcile	The service-profile guard exists only in the postinstall script. Starting a Windows LocalSystem service still writes the Skill into the service profile. Move the guard into reconcilePalaceSkill.
N10	ProcessDriver response for a cancelled request	A PLUGIN_* fault error that comes back from cancelled work is silently discarded. It should be treated as a fault.
—	plugins list	It now runs upgrade-intent recovery, which can roll back, even though list reads as a read-only command. Document this or move it to repair.
—	legacy lock bodies	Locks without a namespace fail closed forever, and the message gives no manual step. Add a remediation (check the PID, then delete the file).
